\CommentTok{\#Risk estimates}
\CommentTok{\#Source: A Quantitative Risk Assessment of Human Salmonellosis from Consumption of Pistachios in the United States}
\CommentTok{\#SOFIA M. SANTILLANA FARAKOS}

\CommentTok{\#Pistachio risk per serving for 4 and 5 log reductions}

\CommentTok{\#Sinker medians}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_4\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{3.63}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{12}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_5\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{3.63}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{13}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_0\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{3.43}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{8}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_sinker\_4\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{2.26}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{18}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_sinker\_5\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{2.26}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{19}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_sinker\_0\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{2.14}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{14}

\CommentTok{\#Floater medians  }
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_4\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{9.49}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{12}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_5\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{9.49}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{13}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_0\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{9.07}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{08}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_floater\_4\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{5.95}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{18}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_floater\_5\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{5.95}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{19}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_floater\_0\_log\_treat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{5.73}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{14}

\CommentTok{\#Sinker means}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_4\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{4.94}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{11}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_5\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{4.86}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{12}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_0\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{4.88}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{7}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_sinker\_4\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{3.07}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{17}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_sinker\_5\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{3.04}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{18}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_sinker\_0\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{3.05}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{13}

\CommentTok{\#Floater means  }
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_4\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{6.23}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{10}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_5\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{6.23}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{11}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_0\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{6.24}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{6}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_floater\_4\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{3.96}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{16}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_floater\_5\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{3.94}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{17}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_ingredient\_cooked\_floater\_0\_log\_treat\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{4.03}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}{-}}\DecValTok{12}

\CommentTok{\#serving sizes}
\NormalTok{serving\_size\_g\_core\_product\_not\_cooked }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{35.6} \CommentTok{\#g pistachios, use core product}
\NormalTok{serving\_size\_g\_ingredient\_cooked }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{3.1} \CommentTok{\#g pistachios}

\CommentTok{\#proportion each type}
\NormalTok{proportion\_sinker }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.85}
\NormalTok{proportion\_floater }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.15}

\CommentTok{\#calculate combined risk, medians}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_0\_log }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_sinker}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_0\_log\_treat) }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_floater}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_0\_log\_treat)}

\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_4\_log }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_sinker}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_4\_log\_treat) }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_floater}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_4\_log\_treat)}

\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_5\_log }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_sinker}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_5\_log\_treat) }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_floater}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_5\_log\_treat)}

\CommentTok{\#calculate combined risk, means}
\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_0\_log\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_sinker}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_0\_log\_treat\_mean) }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_floater}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_0\_log\_treat\_mean)}

\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_4\_log\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_sinker}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_4\_log\_treat\_mean) }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_floater}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_4\_log\_treat\_mean)}

\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_5\_log\_mean }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_sinker}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_sinker\_5\_log\_treat\_mean) }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ (proportion\_floater}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{risk\_per\_serve\_core\_product\_uncooked\_floater\_5\_log\_treat\_mean)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#recall size}
\NormalTok{sales\_original }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{40000000}\SpecialCharTok{*}\FloatTok{0.453592}  \CommentTok{\#kg per year}
\NormalTok{sales\_original\_other }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{40000000}\SpecialCharTok{*}\FloatTok{0.453592}  \CommentTok{\#kg per year}
\NormalTok{recall\_size\_rate }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.325}
\NormalTok{recall\_size\_kg }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ recall\_size\_rate}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{sales\_original }\CommentTok{\#kg of pistachios recalled, same as almonds}
\NormalTok{recall\_size\_g }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ recall\_size\_kg}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{1000}

\NormalTok{servings\_core\_product\_uncooked }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{3.7}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}}\DecValTok{8}
\NormalTok{servings\_ingredient\_cooked }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{6.4}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}}\DecValTok{7}
\NormalTok{total\_servings\_us }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ servings\_core\_product\_uncooked }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ servings\_ingredient\_cooked}
\NormalTok{proportion\_servings\_core\_product\_uncooked }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ servings\_core\_product\_uncooked}\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{total\_servings\_us}
\NormalTok{proportion\_servings\_ingredient\_cooked }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ servings\_ingredient\_cooked}\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{total\_servings\_us}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#cost estimate}
\NormalTok{number\_years }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{1} \CommentTok{\#years, set to 1 because NPV calculation calculates cash flow each year}

\CommentTok{\#create initial for loop}
\NormalTok{cost\_capital\_tech }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{5000000} \CommentTok{\#dollars \#cost of heat treatment tech, if RTE system. In NPV formula, this is C0}

\NormalTok{cost\_sanitation\_tech }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{20000}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{number\_years }\CommentTok{\#dollars}
\NormalTok{cost\_utilities\_tech }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{10000}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{number\_years }\CommentTok{\#dollars}
\NormalTok{cost\_consumables\_tech }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{100000}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{number\_years }\CommentTok{\#dollars}
\NormalTok{cost\_operating\_tech }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_sanitation\_tech }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_utilities\_tech }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_consumables\_tech }\CommentTok{\#total cost operating tech, dollars}

\CommentTok{\#calculating lost product {-} using unit value (assuming that this is what the farm would get paid for the almonds)}
\NormalTok{amt\_product\_recalled }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{  recall\_size\_kg  }\CommentTok{\#kg}
\NormalTok{unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{6.58}\SpecialCharTok{/}\FloatTok{0.453592}  \CommentTok{\#dollars per kg, unit values US shelled almonds exports}
\NormalTok{price\_original }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ unit\_value}
\NormalTok{cost\_lost\_product }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ amt\_product\_recalled}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{price\_original }\CommentTok{\#dollars}

\CommentTok{\#calculate restocking fees}
\NormalTok{destroy\_fee }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{50}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{1323}\NormalTok{)}\SpecialCharTok{/}\FloatTok{0.453592}  \CommentTok{\#dollars per kg}
\NormalTok{restock\_factor }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.5} \CommentTok{\#portion of product recalled that is on shelves and must be restocked}
\NormalTok{amt\_product\_restock }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ recall\_size\_kg}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{restock\_factor }\CommentTok{\#kg, assume all product}
\NormalTok{restocking\_fee }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{300} \CommentTok{\#dollars, flat rate per store}
\NormalTok{number\_stores }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{5000} \CommentTok{\#stores where product is sold (estimation)}
\NormalTok{cost\_restocking1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ destroy\_fee}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{amt\_product\_restock }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ restocking\_fee}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{number\_stores  }\CommentTok{\#dollars}
\NormalTok{spillover\_rate }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.075} \CommentTok{\#portion of product, compared to recalled product, that is not affected by the recall but still restocked}
\NormalTok{cost\_restocking }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_restocking1 }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ spillover\_rate}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{cost\_restocking1 }\CommentTok{\#account for spillover }
\NormalTok{holding\_fee }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{1.5}\SpecialCharTok{/}\FloatTok{0.453592} \CommentTok{\#dollars per kg}
\NormalTok{holding\_rate }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.05} \CommentTok{\#portion of product, compared to recalled product, that is not affected by the recall but is held in a storage facility}
\NormalTok{cost\_holding\_product }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ holding\_fee}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(holding\_rate}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{amt\_product\_restock) }\CommentTok{\#dollars, batch that is a portion of the restocked amount is not recalled, but must be held somewhere.}
\NormalTok{cost\_restocking }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_restocking }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_holding\_product }\CommentTok{\#include holding costs with restocking}

\CommentTok{\#cost litigation}
\NormalTok{cost\_litigation }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{5000000} \CommentTok{\#dollars, assume large recall}

\CommentTok{\#cost crisis management}
\NormalTok{cost\_crisis\_management }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{15000} \CommentTok{\#dollars}

\CommentTok{\#cost enhanced product testing}
\NormalTok{number\_years\_ept }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{1} \CommentTok{\#years}
\NormalTok{cost\_consumables\_ept }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{10000}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{number\_years\_ept }\CommentTok{\#dollars}
\NormalTok{cost\_utilities\_ept }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{10000}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{number\_years\_ept }\CommentTok{\#dollars}
\NormalTok{cost\_ept }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0} \CommentTok{\#cost is negligible.}



\CommentTok{\#calculate change in demand for product}

\NormalTok{sale\_decrease\_after\_recall }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.175} \CommentTok{\#\% decrease (e.g., 0.2 = 20\%) }
\NormalTok{sale\_decreased\_period }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{20}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{365} \CommentTok{\#years}
\NormalTok{sales\_after\_recall }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ sale\_decrease\_after\_recall)}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{sales\_original }\CommentTok{\#kg/year}
\NormalTok{price\_decrease }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0} 
\NormalTok{price\_new }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ price\_decrease)}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{price\_original}
\NormalTok{cost\_demandchange\_product }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sales\_original}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{price\_new}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{sale\_decrease\_after\_recall}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{sale\_decreased\_period }\CommentTok{\#dollars, loss of sales/value due to demand change.}


\CommentTok{\#calculate change in demand for related/other products }

\NormalTok{price\_original\_other }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ unit\_value }\CommentTok{\#dollars per kg}
\NormalTok{sale\_decrease\_after\_recall\_other }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.1} \CommentTok{\#\% decrease (e.g., 0.2 = 20\%)}
\NormalTok{sale\_decreased\_period\_other }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{20}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{365} \CommentTok{\#years}
\NormalTok{sales\_after\_recall\_other }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ sale\_decrease\_after\_recall\_other)}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{sales\_original\_other }\CommentTok{\#kg/year}
\NormalTok{price\_decrease\_other }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0} 
\NormalTok{price\_new\_other }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ price\_decrease\_other)}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{price\_original\_other }\CommentTok{\#dollars per kg}
\NormalTok{cost\_demandchange\_otherproduct }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sales\_original\_other}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{price\_new\_other}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{sale\_decrease\_after\_recall\_other}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{sale\_decreased\_period\_other }\CommentTok{\#dollars}


\CommentTok{\#calculate cost temporary lost production}
\NormalTok{plantdowntime }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{7}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{365} \CommentTok{\#years}
\NormalTok{production\_rate\_downtime }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sales\_original }\CommentTok{\#kg/year}
\NormalTok{cost\_temp\_lost\_production }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ plantdowntime}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{production\_rate\_downtime}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{price\_original }\CommentTok{\#dollars}


\CommentTok{\#traceability1 cost}
\NormalTok{cost\_traceability1\_capital }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0} \CommentTok{\#dollars}
\NormalTok{cost\_traceability1\_utilities }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{number\_years }\CommentTok{\#dollars}
\NormalTok{cost\_traceability1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0} \CommentTok{\#Cost assumed to be negligible }


\CommentTok{\#costs recall insurance (and cost negations)}
\NormalTok{prevalence\_recallins }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0} \CommentTok{\#either 0 or 1}
\NormalTok{cost\_recallins\_premium }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{600000} \CommentTok{\#dollars per year}
\NormalTok{cost\_recallins\_premium }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ prevalence\_recallins}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{cost\_recallins\_premium}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{number\_years}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#switchover point analysis, recall size}

\NormalTok{recall\_size\_kg\_switchover }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{seq}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{from =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{to =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{length.out =} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\#create range to represent \% of production recalled}

\CommentTok{\#sub switchover vector into calculations to determine effect}

\CommentTok{\#cost lost product}
\NormalTok{recall\_size\_kg\_switchover\_amt }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ recall\_size\_kg\_switchover}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{sales\_original}
\NormalTok{cost\_lost\_product\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ recall\_size\_kg\_switchover\_amt}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{price\_original }

\CommentTok{\#cost restocking/holding product}
\NormalTok{amt\_product\_restock\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ recall\_size\_kg\_switchover\_amt}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{restock\_factor}

\NormalTok{cost\_restocking1\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ destroy\_fee}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{amt\_product\_restock\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ restocking\_fee}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{number\_stores  }

\NormalTok{cost\_restocking\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_restocking1\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ spillover\_rate}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{cost\_restocking1\_switchover\_recall\_size }\CommentTok{\#accounts for spillover rate}

\NormalTok{cost\_holding\_product\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ holding\_fee}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(holding\_rate}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{amt\_product\_restock\_switchover\_recall\_size) }\CommentTok{\#dollars, assumes a batch that is \textasciitilde{}5{-}10\% the size of the restocked amount is not recalled, but must be held somewhere.}

\NormalTok{cost\_restocking\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_restocking\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_holding\_product\_switchover\_recall\_size}


\CommentTok{\#recall cost}
\NormalTok{recallcost\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_lost\_product\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_restocking\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_litigation }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_crisis\_management }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_ept }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_demandchange\_product }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_demandchange\_otherproduct }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_temp\_lost\_production }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_recallins\_premium  }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ benefits\_recallins}
\NormalTok{recallcost\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(recall\_size\_kg\_switchover\_amt }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, recallcost\_switchover\_recall\_size)}


\CommentTok{\#recall cost, no illness}
\NormalTok{recallcost\_noillnesses\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ noillness\_recall\_factor}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(cost\_crisis\_management }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_ept }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_demandchange\_product }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_demandchange\_otherproduct) }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_recallins\_premium }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_lost\_product\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_restocking\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_temp\_lost\_production  }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ benefits\_recallins}


\NormalTok{recallcost\_noillnesses\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(recall\_size\_kg\_switchover\_amt }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, recallcost\_noillnesses\_switchover\_recall\_size)}


\CommentTok{\#EV traceability}
\NormalTok{EV\_traceability1\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_outbreaktraced\_trace1}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(recallcost\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_traceability1) }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_outbreaktraced\_trace1)}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{cost\_traceability1 }\CommentTok{\# dollars, expected value of node where outbreak is traced to company or not}


\CommentTok{\#EV illness linked to outbreak, all treatments}

\NormalTok{EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_0log\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_illness\_linked\_to\_outbreak\_0log}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_traceability1\_switchover\_recall\_size) }

\NormalTok{EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_4log\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_illness\_linked\_to\_outbreak\_4log}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_traceability1\_switchover\_recall\_size) }

\NormalTok{EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_5log\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_illness\_linked\_to\_outbreak\_5log}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_traceability1\_switchover\_recall\_size) }


\CommentTok{\#EV illness: note that EV illness not linked to outbreak is not adjusted for switchover because it is p(illness not linke), which is 0, times $0}
\NormalTok{EV\_illness\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (annualrisk\_0log\_median)}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_0log\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illnessnotlinked\_outbreak\_0log)}

\NormalTok{EV\_illness\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (annualrisk\_4log\_median)}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_4log\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illnessnotlinked\_outbreak\_4log)}

\NormalTok{EV\_illness\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (annualrisk\_5log\_median)}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_5log\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illnessnotlinked\_outbreak\_5log)}


\CommentTok{\#EV contam not detected, with switchover vectors}
\NormalTok{EV\_contam\_notdetected\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_test\_contam\_0log)}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illness\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_noillness\_0log\_median)}

\NormalTok{EV\_contam\_notdetected\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_test\_contam\_4log)}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illness\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_noillness\_4log\_median)}

\NormalTok{EV\_contam\_notdetected\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_test\_contam\_5log)}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illness\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_noillness\_5log\_median)}


\CommentTok{\#EV recall no illness}
\NormalTok{EV\_recall\_noillness\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ recallcost\_noillnesses\_switchover\_recall\_size }\CommentTok{\#dollars}

\NormalTok{EV\_recall\_noillness\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ recallcost\_noillnesses\_switchover\_recall\_size }\CommentTok{\#dollars}

\NormalTok{EV\_recall\_noillness\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ recallcost\_noillnesses\_switchover\_recall\_size }\CommentTok{\#dollars}


\CommentTok{\#EV illness no recall}
\NormalTok{EV\_illness\_norecall\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_illness\_norecall\_0log\_median}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_0log\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illnessnotlinked\_outbreak\_0log)}

\NormalTok{EV\_illness\_norecall\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_illness\_norecall\_4log\_median}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_4log\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illnessnotlinked\_outbreak\_4log)}

\NormalTok{EV\_illness\_norecall\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_illness\_norecall\_5log\_median}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_5log\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illnessnotlinked\_outbreak\_5log)}


\CommentTok{\#EV no recall}
\NormalTok{EV\_norecall\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_norecall\_otherint }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illness\_norecall\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size  }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_noillness\_0log\_median}

\NormalTok{EV\_norecall\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_norecall\_otherint }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illness\_norecall\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size  }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_noillness\_4log\_median}

\NormalTok{EV\_norecall\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_norecall\_otherint }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illness\_norecall\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size  }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_noillness\_5log\_median}


\CommentTok{\#calculating contam\_detect\_test and EV\_contam\_detect test. decision tree must choose \textquotesingle{}best decision\textquotesingle{} (least expensive) using switchover vector}

\DocumentationTok{\#\#0 log}
\CommentTok{\# Step 1: Calculate the difference A {-} B}
\NormalTok{difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_recall\_noillness\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_norecall\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size}

\CommentTok{\# Step 2: Count the number of negative differences}
\NormalTok{negative\_count\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\#when the difference is negative, no recall is the worse decision (higher EV)}

\CommentTok{\# Step 3: Check if the difference is negative more than 50\% of the time (i.e., 50 out of 100 times)}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (negative\_count\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(EV\_recall\_noillness\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) \{}
  \CommentTok{\# If difference is negative more than 50\% of the time, multiply vector A by p}
\NormalTok{  EV\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_recall\_noillness\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{p\_test\_contam\_0log}
\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
  \CommentTok{\# Otherwise, multiply vector B by p}
\NormalTok{ EV\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_norecall\_0log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{p\_test\_contam\_0log}
\NormalTok{\}}


\CommentTok{\#4 log}

\CommentTok{\# Step 1: Calculate the difference A {-} B}
\NormalTok{difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_recall\_noillness\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_norecall\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size}

\CommentTok{\# Step 2: Count the number of negative differences}
\NormalTok{negative\_count\_contam\_detect\_test\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\#when the difference is negative, no recall is the worse decision (higher EV)}

\CommentTok{\# Step 3: Check if the difference is negative more than 50\% of the time (i.e., 50 out of 100 times)}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (negative\_count\_contam\_detect\_test\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(EV\_recall\_noillness\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) \{}
  \CommentTok{\# If difference is negative more than 50\% of the time, multiply vector A by p}
\NormalTok{  EV\_contam\_detect\_test\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_recall\_noillness\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{p\_test\_contam\_4log}
\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
  \CommentTok{\# Otherwise, multiply vector B by p}
\NormalTok{ EV\_contam\_detect\_test\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_norecall\_4log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{p\_test\_contam\_4log}
\NormalTok{\}}


\DocumentationTok{\#\# 5 log}
\CommentTok{\# Step 1: Calculate the difference A {-} B}
\NormalTok{difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_recall\_noillness\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_norecall\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size}

\CommentTok{\# Step 2: Count the number of negative differences}
\NormalTok{negative\_count\_contam\_detect\_test\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\#when the difference is negative, no recall is the worse decision (higher EV)}

\CommentTok{\# Step 3: Check if the difference is negative more than 50\% of the time (i.e., 50 out of 100 times)}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (negative\_count\_contam\_detect\_test\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(EV\_recall\_noillness\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) \{}
  \CommentTok{\# If difference is negative more than 50\% of the time, multiply vector A by p}
\NormalTok{  EV\_contam\_detect\_test\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_recall\_noillness\_5log\_median\_switchover\_recall\_size}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{p\_test\_contam\_5log}
\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}

\DocumentationTok{\#\#calculating contam\_detect\_test and EV\_contam\_detect test. decision tree must choose best decision (lowest cost) using switchover vector}

\DocumentationTok{\#\#0 log}
\CommentTok{\# Step 1: Calculate the difference A {-} B}
\NormalTok{difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_mean\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_recall\_noillness\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_norecall\_0log\_mean\_switchover\_recall\_size}

\CommentTok{\# Step 2: Count the number of negative differences}
\NormalTok{negative\_count\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_mean\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_mean\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\#when the difference is negative, no recall is the worse decision (higher EV)}

\CommentTok{\# Step 3: Check if the difference is negative more than 50\% of the time (i.e., 50 out of 100 times)}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (negative\_count\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_mean\_switchover\_recall\_size }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(EV\_recall\_noillness\_switchover\_recall\_size) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) \{}
  \CommentTok{\# If difference is negative more than 50\% of the time, multiply vector A by p}
\NormalTok{  EV\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_mean\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_recall\_noillness\_switchover\_recall\_size}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{p\_test\_contam\_0log}
\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
  \CommentTok{\# Otherwise, multiply vector B by p}
\NormalTok{ EV\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_mean\_switchover\_recall\_size  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_norecall\_0log\_mean\_switchover\_recall\_size}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{p\_test\_contam\_0log}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Step 2: Count the number of negative differences}

\CommentTok{\# Define the calculation function}
\NormalTok{calculate\_cashflow\_5log\_mean\_switchover\_recall\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{() \{}
\NormalTok{  result }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{numeric}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{)  }\CommentTok{\# Initialize an empty vector of length 20, result is the cash flow vector}
  
  \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (t }\ControlFlowTok{in} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{) \{}
    \CommentTok{\# Apply special calculation for the first item}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (t }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{      result[t] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_capital\_tech}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{extra\_reduction\_cost\_factor[}\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{] }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_training\_tech }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ extra\_reduction\_cost\_factor[}\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{]}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{cost\_operating\_tech }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cashflow\_5log\_mean1\_switchover\_recall\_size[i] }\CommentTok{\# Adjust formula for first item}

\NormalTok{    \}}

\NormalTok{      result[t] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ result[t] }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ cost\_training\_tech  }\CommentTok{\# Add staff training cost every 4th year, cost training tech already defined}
\NormalTok{    \}}
\NormalTok{  \}}
  
  \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(result)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{EV\_recall\_noillness\_5log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ recallcost\_noillnesses\_switchover\_unit\_value }\CommentTok{\#dollars}

\CommentTok{\#EV illness no recall}
\NormalTok{EV\_illness\_norecall\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_illness\_norecall\_0log\_median}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illnessnotlinked\_outbreak\_0log)}

\NormalTok{EV\_illness\_norecall\_4log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_illness\_norecall\_4log\_median}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_4log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illnessnotlinked\_outbreak\_4log)}

\NormalTok{EV\_illness\_norecall\_5log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p\_illness\_norecall\_5log\_median}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{(EV\_illnesslinked\_outbreak\_5log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illnessnotlinked\_outbreak\_5log)}


\CommentTok{\#EV no recall}
\NormalTok{EV\_norecall\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_norecall\_otherint\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illness\_norecall\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_noillness\_0log\_median}

\NormalTok{EV\_norecall\_4log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_norecall\_otherint\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illness\_norecall\_4log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_noillness\_4log\_median}

\NormalTok{EV\_norecall\_5log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cost\_norecall\_otherint\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_illness\_norecall\_5log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ EV\_noillness\_5log\_median}


\DocumentationTok{\#\#contam detect test}
\DocumentationTok{\#\#calculating contam\_detect\_test and EV\_contam\_detect test. decision model must choose best decision using switchover vector}

\DocumentationTok{\#\#0 log}
\CommentTok{\# Step 1: Calculate the difference A {-} B}
\NormalTok{difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_recall\_noillness\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_norecall\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value}

\CommentTok{\# Step 2: Count the number of negative differences}
\NormalTok{negative\_count\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\#when the difference is negative, no recall is the worse decision (higher EV)}

\CommentTok{\# Step 3: Check if the difference is negative more than 50\% of the time (i.e., 50 out of 100 times)}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (negative\_count\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(EV\_recall\_noillness\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) \{}
  \CommentTok{\# If difference is negative more than 50\% of the time, multiply vector A by p}
\NormalTok{  EV\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_recall\_noillness\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{p\_test\_contam\_0log}
\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
  \CommentTok{\# Otherwise, multiply vector B by p}
\NormalTok{ EV\_contam\_detect\_test\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_norecall\_0log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value}\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{p\_test\_contam\_0log}
\NormalTok{\}}


\DocumentationTok{\#\#4 log}
\CommentTok{\# Step 1: Calculate the difference A {-} B}
\NormalTok{difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_4log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_recall\_noillness\_4log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ EV\_norecall\_4log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value}

\CommentTok{\# Step 2: Count the number of negative differences}
\NormalTok{negative\_count\_contam\_detect\_test\_4log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(difference\_contam\_detect\_test\_4log\_median\_switchover\_unit\_value }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\#when the difference is negative, no recall is the worse decision (higher EV)}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#switchover, cost of capital tech}
\NormalTok{cost\_capital\_tech\_switchover }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{seq}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{from =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{to =} \DecValTok{25000000}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{length.out =} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\#sum total cost {-} everything}
